The null hypothesis is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the expected number of rolls showing face 6 under the null hypothesis? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>36/6 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>36/5 =</span> <strong>7.2</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>36/2 =</span> <strong>18</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>36/1 =</span> <strong>36</strong>	Incorrect
